<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-35896D91-A3BE-4BE3-9135-008AD443B921'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What is the difference between the largest and the smallest of the following numbers:

$0.9 \quad  0.17 \quad  0.72 \quad  0.73$

- $0.9$
- $0.17$
- $0.72$
- $0.73$ 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Largest number}           &= 0.9  \\\\
\text{Smallest number}          &= 0.17 \\\\
\text{Difference}               &= 0.9 - 0.17 \\\\
                                &= 0.73
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='option'>
<p>D</p>
</div>
<div class='answer'>

$0.73$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Largest number}           &= 0.9  \\\\
\text{Smallest number}          &= 0.17 \\\\
\text{Difference}               &= 0.9 - 0.17 \\\\
                                &= 0.73
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>D</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$0.73$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question question'>

- $1021$
- $1234$
- $1023$

</div>
</div>

</div>
</div>

</code></pre>

</code></pre>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-376FC516-ED40-42F7-AC7C-A63AECDD668C'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What is $(999 - 99 + 9) \div 9$?

- $99$
- $100$
- $101$
- $109$ 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: Using BIDMAS
,

$
\begin{aligned}
&= (999 - 99 + 9) \div 9 \\\\
&= (999 - 90) \div 9 \\\\
&= \dfrac {909} {9} \\\\
&= \dfrac {9 \times 101} {9} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel{9} \times 101} {\cancel{9}} \\\\
&= 101
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='option'>
<p>C</p>
</div>
<div class='answer'>

$101$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_bidmas,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
&= (999 - 99 + 9) \div 9 \\\\
&= (999 - 90) \div 9 \\\\
&= \dfrac {909} {9} \\\\
&= \dfrac {9 \times 101} {9} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel{9} \times 101} {\cancel{9}} \\\\
&= 101
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>C</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$101$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-B4401778-D5A5-43C6-92E1-D7C3D77AF227'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

The number $987654321$ is multiplied by $9$.
How many times does the digit $8$ occur in the result?

- $1$
- $2$
- $3$
- $9$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Multiplication}       &= 987654321 \times 9 \\\\
                            &= 8888888889 \\\\
\text{Count}                &= 9
\end{aligned}
$


</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='option'>
<p>D</p>
</div>
<div class='answer'>

$9$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Multiplication}       &= 987654321 \times 9 \\\\
                            &= 8888888889 \\\\
\text{Count}                &= 9
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>D</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$9$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-AB7057B1-8C72-4023-A583-CA2D1B61CFD9'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Which of the following has the same remainder when it is divided by $2$
as when it is divided by $3$?

- $5$
- $7$
- $9$
- $11$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

As per the question,

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-2DF6D85D-B0AA-476C-BAA9-7CFAFB3CB5CB'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What is the value of $19 + 99 + 19 \times 99$?

- $1999$
- $11701$
- $13563$
- None of these 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: Using BIDMAS
,

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 19 + 99 + 19 \times 99 \\\\
&= 19 + 99 \times (1 + 19) \\\\
&= 19 + 99 \times 20 \\\\
&= 19 + 1980 \\\\
&= 1999
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='option'>
<p>A</p>
</div>
<div class='answer'>

$1999$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_bidmas,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
&= 19 + 99 + 19 \times 99 \\\\
&= 19 + 99 \times (1 + 19) \\\\
&= 19 + 99 \times 20 \\\\
&= 19 + 1980 \\\\
&= 1999
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>A</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$1999$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-601C328B-929D-4CC4-9443-D89375DA371E'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Which of this calculation produces a multiple of $5$?

- $1 + 2 \times 3 + 4$
- $1 \times 2 + 3 \times 4$
- $1 + 2 \times 3 \times 4$
- $1 \times 2 \times 3 \times 4$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&\text {a.} \quad   1 + 2 \times 3 + 4 &= 11 \not = {\text{multiple of}} \ 5 \\\\
&\text {b.} \quad   1 \times 2 + 3 \times 4 &= 14 \not = {\text{multiple of}} \ 5 \\\\
&\text {c.} \quad   1 + 2 \times 3 \times 4 &= 25 = {\text{multiple of}} \ 5 \\\\
&\text {d.} \quad   1 \times 2 \times 3 \times 4 &= 24 \not = {\text{multiple of}} \ 5
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='option'>
<p>C</p>
</div>
<div class='answer'>

$1 + 2 \times 3 \times 4$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&\text {a.} \quad   1 + 2 \times 3 + 4 &= 11 \not = {\text{multiple of}} \ 5 \\\\
&\text {b.} \quad   1 \times 2 + 3 \times 4 &= 14 \not = {\text{multiple of}} \ 5 \\\\
&\text {c.} \quad   1 + 2 \times 3 \times 4 &= 25 = {\text{multiple of}} \ 5 \\\\
&\text {d.} \quad   1 \times 2 \times 3 \times 4 &= 24 \not = {\text{multiple of}} \ 5
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>C</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$1 + 2 \times 3 \times 4$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

DONE a b c d small.

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-91DE3197-B618-4BD0-A795-29B2AC55AF03'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Which of the following is divisible by all of the numbers from $1$ to $10$ inclusive?

- $34 \times 45$
- $45 \times 56$
- $56 \times 27$
- $67 \times 78$ 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$45 \times 56$

Lets verify.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{A} &= 34 \times 45 \\\\
         &= 1530 \\\\
\text{B} &= 45 \times 56 \\\\
         &= 2520 \\\\
\text{A} &= 56 \times 27 \\\\
         &= 1512 \\\\
\text{A} &= 67 \times 78 \\\\
         &= 5226 
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$45 \times 56$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$45 \times 56$
<emptyline>

<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{A} &= 34 \times 45 \\\\
         &= 1530 \\\\
\text{B} &= 45 \times 56 \\\\
         &= 2520 \\\\
\text{A} &= 56 \times 27 \\\\
         &= 1512 \\\\
\text{A} &= 67 \times 78 \\\\
         &= 5226 
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$45 \times 56$
</code></pre>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-F1298F25-DACE-45D0-91EE-A49F30843ED5'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Which one of these calculations is **incorrect**?

- $4 \times 5 + 67 = 45 + 6 \times 7$
- $3 \times 7 + 48 = 37 + 4 \times 8$
- $2 \times 5 + 69 = 25 + 6 \times 9$
- $9 \times 6 + 73 = 96 + 7 \times 3$ 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: Using BIDMAS
,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {A. First value }    &= 4 \times 5 + 67 \\\\
                           &= 87 \\\\
\text{Second value}        &= 45 + 6 \times 7 \\\\
                           &= \green
{87} \\\\
\text {B. First value }    &= 3 \times 7 + 48 \\\\
                           &= 69 \\\\
\text{Second value}        &= 37 + 4 \times 8 \\\\                         
                           &= \green
{69} \\\\
\text {C. First value }    &= 2 \times 5 + 69 \\\\
                           &= 79 \\\\                    
\text{Second value}        &= 25 + 6 \times 9 \\\\
                           &= \green
{79} \\\\
\text {D. First value }    &= 9 \times 6 + 73 \\\\
                           &= 127 \\\\                    
\text{Second value}        &= 96 + 7 \times 3 \\\\
                           &= \red
{117} \\\\
\maroonC
{127} &\neq \maroonC
{117}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='option'>
<p>D</p>
</div>
<div class='answer'>

$9 \times 6 + 73 = 96 + 7 \times 3$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_bidmas,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text {A. First value }    &= 4 \times 5 + 67 \\\\
                           &= 87 \\\\
\text{Second value}        &= 45 + 6 \times 7 \\\\
                           &= \rtq_katex_color_answer{87} \\\\
\text {B. First value }    &= 3 \times 7 + 48 \\\\
                           &= 69 \\\\
\text{Second value}        &= 37 + 4 \times 8 \\\\                         
                           &= \rtq_katex_color_answer{69} \\\\
\text {C. First value }    &= 2 \times 5 + 69 \\\\
                           &= 79 \\\\                    
\text{Second value}        &= 25 + 6 \times 9 \\\\
                           &= \rtq_katex_color_answer{79} \\\\
\text {D. First value }    &= 9 \times 6 + 73 \\\\
                           &= 127 \\\\                    
\text{Second value}        &= 96 + 7 \times 3 \\\\
                           &= \rtq_katex_color_wrong_answer{117} \\\\
\rtq_katex_color_info{127} &\neq \rtq_katex_color_info{117}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>D</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$9 \times 6 + 73 = 96 + 7 \times 3$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

On a very long straight road there are four villages: Allwood, Bentham, Corford and Dunbar, which are shown in the diagram below.

![missing image](/papers/missing_image.svg)


The arrows show distances between villages.

Find the distances from

<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Corford to Dunbar

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Corford to Dunbar}                         &= \text{Bentham to Dunbar} - \text{Bentham to Corford} \\\\
                                                 &= 18 \ \text{km} - 4 \ \text{km} \\\\
                                                 &= 14 \ \text{km}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$14 \ \text{km}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Corford to Dunbar}                         &= \text{Bentham to Dunbar} - \text{Bentham to Corford} \\\\
                                                 &= 18 \ \text{km} - 4 \ \text{km} \\\\
                                                 &= 14 \ \text{km}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>

Allwood to Dunbar

</div>
<div class='workings'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Allwood to Dunbar}                         &= \text{Allwood to Corford} + \text{Corford to Dunbar} \\\\
                                                 &= 12 \ \text{km} + 14 \ \text{km} \\\\
                                                 &= 26 \ \text{km}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>

&= \dfrac {2304 \cancel{0}} {1 \cancel{0}} \\\\
&= 2304
\end{aligned}
$

<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Find two numbers which when multiplied together make a hundred. 
Neither of the two numbers must use any $0$’s.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 100 \\\\
&= 2 \times 2 \times 5 \times 5 \\\\
&= 4 \times 25
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$4 \ \text{and} \ 25$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 100 \\\\
&= 2 \times 2 \times 5 \times 5 \\\\
&= 4 \times 25
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>

</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$4 \ \text{and} \ 25$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Once again, neither of the two numbers must use any $0$’s.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 1000 \\\\
&= 100 \times 10 \\\\
&= 4 \times 25 \times 2 \times 5 \\\\
&= 8 \times 125
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>

$8 \ \text{and} \ 125$

<div class='question subquestion'>

Write $\dfrac{3}{4}$ as a percentage.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {3} {4} \times 100 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 100} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 25 \times 4} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 25 \times \cancel {4}} {\cancel {4}} \\\\
&= 3 \times 25 \\\\
&= 75 \%
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

&= \dfrac {3 \times 25 \times \cancel {4}} {\cancel {4}} \\\\

\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {8738} {25.7} \\\\
&= \dfrac {8738 \times 10} {257}  \\\\

&= \dfrac {257 \times 34 \times 10} {257}  \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {257} \times 34 \times 10} {\cancel {257}}  \\\\
&= 34 \times 10 \\\\
&= 340
\end{aligned}
$

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-FCB6CEA1-F7E6-4747-8C98-E15D79B8A2AE'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What number is halfway between $-3$ and $8$? 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times (-3 + 8) \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times 5\\\\
&= \dfrac {5} {2} \\\\
&= 2.5
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$2.5$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times (-3 + 8) \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times 5\\\\
&= \dfrac {5} {2} \\\\
&= 2.5
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$2.5$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-5757E4AA-C7E9-4186-8F69-0AA3D64752CA'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

$0.126$ is halfway between two numbers.

One of the numbers is $0.11$.

What is the other number? 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let the other number $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\dfrac {1}{2} \times (0.11 + a)     &= 0.126 \\\\
0.11 + a                             &= 0.126 \times 2 \\\\
0.11 + a                             &= 0.252 \\\\
a                                    &= 0.252 - 0.11 \\\\
a                                    &= 0.142
\end{aligned}
$

Other number $= 0.142$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$0.142$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Let the other number $= a$
<emptyline>
As per the question,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\dfrac {1}{2} \times (0.11 + a)     &= 0.126 \\\\
0.11 + a                             &= 0.126 \times 2 \\\\
0.11 + a                             &= 0.252 \\\\
a                                    &= 0.252 - 0.11 \\\\
a                                    &= 0.142
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Other number $= 0.142$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$0.142$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-0A9683A9-D010-44F1-9170-501D9A4DF97D'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

$36$ is a square number because it can be written $6 \times 6$.

Similarly, $64 = 8 \times 8$ is a square number.

$1000$ is a cube number because it can be written $10 \times 10 \times 10$

Similarly, $343 = 7 \times 7 \times 7$ is a cube number.

The French mathematician Pierre de Fermat claimed correctly that there is only one square number that is $2$ less than a cube number.

It is less than $50$.

What is it? 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$25$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$25$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$25$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$25$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-A5529866-6896-4634-A0C7-7BA27DF46995'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What is halfway between $120$ and $300$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>


$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times (120 + 300) \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times 420\\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 210} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {2} \times 210} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
&= 210
\end{aligned}
$


</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$210$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>
$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times (120 + 300) \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times 420\\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 210} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {2} \times 210} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
&= 210
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$210$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

What is $0.32$ as a fraction? Give your answer in its simplest form.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 0.32 \\\\
&= \dfrac {32} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {8 \times 4} {25 \times 4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {8 \times \cancel {4}} {25 \times \cancel {4}} \\\\
&= \dfrac {8} {25}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\dfrac {8} {25}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 0.32 \\\\
&= \dfrac {32} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {8 \times 4} {25 \times 4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {8 \times \cancel {4}} {25 \times \cancel {4}} \\\\
&= \dfrac {8} {25}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\dfrac {8} {25}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>

<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

What is eighteen fortieths as a decimal?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {18} {40} \\\\
&= \dfrac {9 \times 2} {20 \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {9 \times 2 \times 5} {20 \times 2 \times 5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {9 \times \cancel 2 \times 5} {20 \times \cancel 2 \times 5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {45} {100} \\\\
&= 0.45
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$0.45$

</div>
</div>

\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {18} {40} \\\\
&= \dfrac {9 \times 2} {20 \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {9 \times 2 \times 5} {20 \times 2 \times 5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {9 \times \cancel 2 \times 5} {20 \times \cancel 2 \times 5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {45} {100} \\\\
&= 0.45
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-329E7FE5-9B8D-4D16-8082-F798504B6844'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What is $0.2 \times 0.3$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: Let's first multiply the numbers ignoring the decimal points.


$2 \times 3 = 6$

Between the two numbers, the total number of digits after the decimal point is two.

Let's account for the decimal point now. 

$= 0.06$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$0.06$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_multiply_when_decimal_points_note
<emptyline>
$2 \times 3 = 6$
<emptyline>
Between the two numbers, the total number of digits after the decimal point is two.
<emptyline>
Let's account for the decimal point now. 
<emptyline>
$= 0.06$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$0.06$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

How many centimetres are there in $1.4 \ \text{metres}$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 1.4 \ \text {metres} \\\\
&= 1.4 \times 100 \ \text {centimetres} \\\\
&= 140 \ \text {centimetres}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$140 \ \text {centimetres}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 1.4 \ \text {metres} \\\\
&= 1.4 \times 100 \ \text {centimetres} \\\\
&= 140 \ \text {centimetres}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 2.3 \ \text {kilometres} \\\\
&= 2.3 \times 1000 \ \text {meters} \\\\
&= 2.3 \times 1000 \times 1000 \ \text {millimetres} \\\\
&= 2300000 \ \text {millimetres}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

&= 2.3 \times 1000 \times 1000 \ \text {millimetres} \\\\
&= 2300000 \ \text {millimetres}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-E5D37E54-20E2-4DB6-83F2-B10511CE4323'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Jaishan notices that if he squares two numbers and find the difference  of his answer he gets the same result as multiplying the sum and the difference of the two number.

For example, if he chooses $9$ and $3$:   

$9 ^ {2} - 3 ^ {2} = 81 - 9 = 72$ and 

$(9 + 3) \times (9-3) = 12 \times 6 = 72$

Show that this works with the number $6$ and $2$.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Square of numbers}                &= 6 ^ {2} - 2 ^ {2} \\\\
                                        &= 36 - 4 \\\\
                                        &= 32 \\\\
\text{Product of sum \& difference}      &= (6 + 2) \times (6 - 2) \\\\
                                        &= 8 \times 4 \\\\
                                        &= 32
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$32$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Square of numbers}                &= 6 ^ {2} - 2 ^ {2} \\\\
                                        &= 36 - 4 \\\\
                                        &= 32 \\\\
\text{Product of sum \& difference}      &= (6 + 2) \times (6 - 2) \\\\
                                        &= 8 \times 4 \\\\
                                        &= 32
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$32$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Jaishan uses this to find $83 \times 83 - 17 \times 17$ **WITHOUT** using long multiplication.
How did he do it and what is the answer?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Using the above method,

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 83 ^ {2} - 17 ^ {2} \\\\
&= (83 + 17) \times (83 - 17) \\\\
&= 100 \times 56 \\\\
&= 5600
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$5600$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Using the above method,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
&= 83 ^ {2} - 17 ^ {2} \\\\
&= (83 + 17) \times (83 - 17) \\\\
&= 100 \times 56 \\\\
&= 5600
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$5600$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-5944BAB9-F811-4821-A822-19421386ED7E'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

How many grams are there in $0.0025 \ \text{kilograms}$? 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 0.0025 \ \text {kilograms} \\\\
&= 0.0025 \times 1000 \ \text {grams} \\\\
&= 2.5 \ \text {grams}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$2.5 \ \text {grams}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 0.0025 \ \text {kilograms} \\\\
&= 0.0025 \times 1000 \ \text {grams} \\\\
&= 2.5 \ \text {grams}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$2.5 \ \text {grams}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-596C756A-4491-4F11-86D2-84247118FAAC'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Ella noticed that the $5 \text{th October } 2015$ could be written $5{-}10{-}15$, with all numbers being a multiple of $5$.
When is the next date **from today** ($23 \text{rd January } 2016$) which when written in this form also has all the numbers being a multiple of $5$? 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$5 \text{th May} 2020$

Lets verfy.

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 5 \text{th May} 2020 \\\\
&= 5-5-20
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$5 \text{th May} 2020$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$5 \text{th May} 2020$
<emptyline>
Lets verfy.
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
&= 5 \text{th May} 2020 \\\\
&= 5-5-20
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$5 \text{th May} 2020$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-99B747C3-A340-4D5D-B8DF-E82C580210CB'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What is $\dfrac{1}{4}$ of $0.02$? 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {1} {4} \ \text {of} \ 0.02 \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {4} \times 0.02 \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {4} \times \dfrac {20} {1000} \\\\
&= \dfrac {20} {4 \times 1000} \\\\
&= \dfrac {5 \times 4} {1000 \times 4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {5 \times \cancel {4}} {1000 \times \cancel {4}} \\\\
&= \dfrac {5} {1000} \\\\
&= 0.005
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$0.005$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {1} {4} \ \text {of} \ 0.02 \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {4} \times 0.02 \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {4} \times \dfrac {20} {1000} \\\\
&= \dfrac {20} {4 \times 1000} \\\\
&= \dfrac {5 \times 4} {1000 \times 4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {5 \times \cancel {4}} {1000 \times \cancel {4}} \\\\
&= \dfrac {5} {1000} \\\\
&= 0.005
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$0.005$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
